TL;DR: 本文提出ReAlloc框架，用于解决电商平台在多个营销渠道间分配固定预算的决策问题。该问题被建模为单纯形约束的提升（uplift）决策问题，ReAlloc采用快慢因果框架（快速正交教师提取无偏局部梯度，慢速解释引导学生蒸馏为结构化边际场），实现了支持感知的保守决策，能捕捉跨渠道替代效应。在淘宝平台的大规模模拟和线上A/B测试中取得了支付订单和收入的同步提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的预测-优化（PTO）范式在渠道预算分配的合成空间中失效，原因是存在观测混淆偏差和严重的分布外（外推）问题，导致决策不准确。

Method: 提出ReAlloc框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 敏捷正交教师（Orthogonal Teacher）：从短期日志中提取无偏局部梯度；(2) 解释引导学生（Explanation-Guided Student）：将局部梯度蒸馏到长期时间范围内的结构化边际场中。该设计支持跨渠道替代效应的捕捉，并做出支持感知的保守决策。

Result: 通过大规模模拟实验和淘宝平台上的线上A/B测试，ReAlloc在支付订单量和收入两个指标上均实现了同步提升。

Conclusion: ReAlloc框架能够有效克服PTO范式在预算分配中的局限，通过快慢因果学习实现多渠道预算的优化分配，在实际电商平台中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: E-commerce platforms must allocate fixed marketing budgets across multiple channels to maximize business utility. However, standard predict-then-optimize (PTO) paradigms fail in this compositional space due to observational confounding and severe extrapolation. We formulate this challenge as a simplex-constrained uplift decision problem and propose ReAlloc, a fast-slow causal framework. Specifically, an agile Orthogonal Teacher extracts unbiased local gradients from short-term logs, while an Explanation-Guided Student distills them into a structured marginal field over long-term horizons. This design enables support-aware, conservative decisions that capture cross-channel substitutions. Extensive simulations and large-scale online A/B tests on Taobao platform demonstrate that ReAlloc achieves simultaneous lifts in both pay order and income.

</details>


### [93] [Persistent Gaussian Perturbations Prevent Oversmoothing in Recurrent Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28185)
*Mostafa Haghir Chehreghani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种注入高斯噪声的循环图神经网络，将其建模为随机动力系统，证明在全局收缩假设下其隐表示形成几何遍历的马尔可夫链，并给出了平稳Dirichlet能量的正下界，从而严格保证渐近过平滑被抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过平滑是深度图神经网络的根本限制，导致节点表示趋于相似并坍缩到低维子空间，限制了消息传递架构的有效深度。现有解决方案多为确定性方法（残差连接、归一化、图重连），本文探索随机扰动作为对抗过平滑的新机制。

Method: 将每次传播步骤后注入独立高斯噪声的循环GNN建模为随机动力系统；在确定性更新的全局收缩假设下，证明隐表示构成几何遍历的马尔可夫链并具有唯一不变概率测度；通过理论分析推导平稳Dirichlet能量的显式正下界，该下界与噪声方差和图谱间隙成正比。

Result: 理论证明了平稳表示不会坍缩到常值流形，即渐近过平滑在Dirichlet能量非消失的意义下得到严格防止；在线性和非线性循环图神经网络上的数值实验与理论预测高度吻合，验证了平稳分布的出现以及极限Dirichlet能量对噪声强度的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 持久随机扰动是对抗过平滑的一种根本不同机制，补充了现有的确定性方法；该工作为随机性在深度GNN中的作用提供了严格的理论保证，并给出了可验证的实验支持。

Abstract: Oversmoothing is a fundamental limitation of deep graph neural networks (GNNs), where repeated message passing causes node representations to become increasingly similar, eventually collapsing toward a low-dimensional subspace. This phenomenon limits the effective depth of message-passing architectures and motivates the search for mechanisms that preserve representation diversity. In this paper, we study a recurrent graph neural network in which independent Gaussian noise is injected after every propagation step and analyze the resulting architecture as a stochastic dynamical system. Under a standard global contraction assumption on the deterministic update, we prove that the hidden representations form a geometrically ergodic Markov chain admitting a unique invariant probability measure. Our main theoretical result establishes an explicit positive lower bound on the expected stationary Dirichlet energy, proportional to both the noise variance and the spectral gap of the underlying graph. Consequently, the stationary representations cannot collapse onto the constant manifold, providing a rigorous guarantee that asymptotic oversmoothing is prevented in the sense of non-vanishing Dirichlet energy. Our analysis reveals persistent stochastic perturbations as a fundamentally different mechanism for combating oversmoothing, complementing existing deterministic approaches based on residual connections, normalization, and graph rewiring. Finally, numerical experiments on both linear and nonlinear recurrent graph neural networks closely match the theoretical predictions, illustrating the emergence of a stationary distribution and the predicted dependence of the limiting Dirichlet energy on the noise intensity.

</details>


### [94] [TopoFormer: Topology Meets Attention for Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28259)
*Md Joshem Uddin,Astrit Tola,Cuneyt Gurcan Akcora,Baris Coskunuzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Topoformer，一个轻量级且可扩展的图表示学习框架，通过Topo-Scan模块将图的拓扑结构编码为注意力友好的有序序列，由Transformer处理生成图级嵌入，在多个基准上达到或超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持久同调管线计算代价高且难以并行化，难以与标准深度学习架构无缝集成；现有GNN和基于拓扑的方法在表达效率和计算可预测性上存在局限，需要一种既能编码多尺度拓扑结构又具备高效并行计算能力的图表示学习方法。

Method: 提出Topoformer框架，核心是Topo-Scan模块，通过节点或边过滤切片将图分解为短而有序的拓扑token序列，捕获从局部模体到全局组织的多尺度结构模式，再利用Transformer处理这些序列生成图级嵌入。与传统持久同调不同，Topo-Scan可并行化且避免昂贵的图计算。

Result: 在多个图分类和分子性质预测基准上达到最先进性能，匹敌或超越强力的GNN和基于拓扑的基线方法，同时提供可预测且高效的计算开销，并给出了拓扑编码稳定性的理论保证。

Conclusion: 这项工作为图表示学习开辟了新的并行化路径，将拓扑归纳偏置整合到注意力框架中，为统一的、可扩展的图学习方法提供了新方向。

Abstract: We introduce Topoformer, a lightweight and scalable framework for graph representation learning that encodes topological structure into attention-friendly sequences. At the core of our method is Topo-Scan, a novel module that decomposes a graph into a short, ordered sequence of topological tokens by slicing over node or edge filtrations. These sequences capture multi-scale structural patterns, from local motifs to global organization, and are processed by a Transformer to produce expressive graph-level embeddings. Unlike traditional persistent homology pipelines, Topo-Scan is parallelizable, avoids costly diagram computations, and integrates seamlessly with standard deep learning architectures. We provide theoretical guarantees on the stability of our topological encodings and demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across graph classification and molecular property prediction benchmarks. Our results show that Topoformer matches or exceeds strong GNN and topology-based baselines while offering predictable and efficient compute. This work opens a new path for parallelizable and unifying approaches to graph representation learning that integrate topological inductive biases into attention frameworks.

</details>


### [95] [HARGO: Heterogeneity-Aware Reward-Guided Optimization for RL Post-Training of LLMs on HPC Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28301)
*Tiangang Li,Xiangbo Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HARGO是一种面向异构HPC任务的异质性感知奖励引导优化方法，通过置信度调制的优势加权（confidence-modulated advantage）实现逐响应的重要性加权，无需任务标签即可在四个HPC任务上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调（SFT）虽然能为大语言模型注入HPC领域知识（如数据竞争检测、基准测试问答），但仅靠知识无法保证任务恰当的行为。HPC任务存在极端异质性，不同任务的答案长度差异达58倍，奖励分布截然不同，SFT准确率差异大，导致统一权重的RL方法（如GRPO）表现次优。

Method: HARGO提出逐响应重要性加权机制：从组级奖励对比中计算判别信号（discrimination signal），从参考模型对数概率中计算置信度信号（confidence signal），两者共同调制优势值（advantage），再计算每个响应的权重，整个过程无需任务类型标签。

Result: 在四个HPC任务和九种方法对比中，HARGO在所有三个主要指标上均取得最佳表现：WinRate 54.62%、数据竞争F1 91.30%、PLP相似度0.8558。消融实验证实两个信号具有互补贡献。

Conclusion: HARGO在异构HPC任务中建立了最佳的整体对齐质量，验证了异质性感知的奖励引导优化方法比统一权重的RL方法更适合HPC多任务场景。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) can equip large language models (LLMs) with domain knowledge for high-performance computing (HPC) tasks such as data race detection and benchmark question answering. However, knowledge alone does not guarantee task-appropriate behavior: the same SFT model that correctly classifies 88.65\% of C/C++ data race samples produces verbose, imprecise answers to factual queries, with 65.9\% of MLPerf responses exceeding 40 characters. Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training addresses this gap by optimizing for task-specific rewards rather than token-level imitation. Yet HPC tasks exhibit extreme heterogeneity, with binary classification, factual QA, and semantic generation differing by 58x in answer length, spanning three distinct reward distributions, and showing widely varying SFT accuracy. This makes uniform-weight RL methods such as GRPO suboptimal. We propose HARGO, Heterogeneity-Aware Reward-Guided Optimization, which introduces per-response importance weighting via confidence-modulated advantage: computing a discrimination signal from group-level reward contrast and a confidence signal from reference model log-probabilities, then modulating the advantage before computing per-response weights, without requiring task-type labels. Across four HPC tasks and nine methods, HARGO achieves the best performance on all three primary metrics: WinRate 54.62\%, Data Race F1 91.30\%, and PLP Similarity 0.8558. Ablation confirms complementary contributions from both signals. HARGO establishes the best overall alignment quality among compared methods for heterogeneous HPC tasks.

</details>


### [96] [Semi-Supervised Learning for Molecular Graphs via Ensemble Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28304)
*Rasmus Tirsgaard,Laurits Fredsgaard,Marisa Wodrich,Mikkel Jordahn,Mikkel N. Schmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用集成共识的图神经网络半监督学习方法，在无需标签增强的情况下，通过集成一致性的训练目标提升分子性质预测的准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子科学中的标注数据获取成本高昂，而大量未标注分子数据廉价易得。传统半监督学习方法依赖标签保持的数据增强，但在分子领域难以设计——微小的分子结构变化可能导致性质大幅改变，亟需不需要标签增强的半监督方法。

Method: 采用基于集成共识（ensemble consensus）的半监督学习目标，利用多个图神经网络模型对未标注数据的一致性预测作为训练信号，使集成中单个成员模型的预测能力得到增强，并引入类似知识蒸馏的训练效果。

Result: 在多个分子数据集、不同任务类型和图神经网络架构上均验证了该方法能提升预测准确性；单个集成成员在集成共识目标训练后，几乎所有情况下都优于传统监督方式训练的完整集成；同时该方法还降低了模型的校准误差，增强了模型鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于集成共识的半监督学习是分子领域可行的、无需复杂标签增强的策略，能够提升预测性能、鲁棒性和校准质量，为该领域的研究提供了一种实用的半监督训练范式。

Abstract: Machine learning is transforming molecular sciences by accelerating property prediction, simulation, and the discovery of new molecules and materials. Acquiring labeled data in these domains is often costly and time-consuming, whereas large collections of unlabeled molecular data are readily available. Standard semi-supervised learning methods often rely on label-preserving augmentations, which are challenging to design in the molecular domain, where minor changes can drastically alter properties. In this work, we show that semi-supervised methods that rely on an ensemble consensus can boost predictive accuracy across a diverse range of molecular datasets, task types, and graph neural network architectures. We find that training with an ensemble consensus objective increases robustness in models and exhibits an effect similar to knowledge distillation; an individual member of an ensemble trained this way outperforms a full ensemble trained in a traditional supervised fashion in almost all cases. In addition, this type of semi-supervised training reduces calibration error.

</details>


### [97] [Beyond Geometric Complementarity: Coherent Overlap in Sparse Mixture-of-Experts Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28308)
*Huiyuan Tian,Bonan Xu,Shijian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过区分"路由一致性、候选质量、候选与上下文交互"三个概念，提出了专家子空间分离指数（ESSI）、匹配路由残差和前缀控制的2×2因子设计，在六个MoE架构上系统研究了稀疏混合专家模型的路由机制。研究发现专家子空间高度重叠但实际路由仍优于匹配替代方案；虽然选中的专家在解释残差表示上始终优于最强的未选中竞争者，但实际前缀在所有情况下都缩小了这一优势（所有交互均为负）。然而这种几何上的"收窄"并不等于功能冗余——在39个冻结路由比较中，24个显示添加后期专家改善了下游预测。作者将此模式命名为"相干重叠"（coherent overlap），表明几何相似性本身不能决定冗余或剪枝价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏混合专家（MoE）语言模型的核心假设是"被共同选中的专家应贡献不同的表示方向"，但现有证据往往混淆了路由一致性、候选质量和候选与上下文的交互三个不同数量。作者希望厘清这些概念，回答一个关键问题：专家子空间的重叠是否意味着功能冗余？几何相似性能否指导模型剪枝决策？

Method: 本文提出了三个核心工具：(1) 专家子空间分离指数（ESSI）用于量化专家子空间的重叠程度；(2) 匹配路由残差用于分离路由选择本身的影响；(3) 前缀控制的2×2因子设计用于区分候选质量和候选与上下文的交互。此外采用冻结路由干预和受控Top-k训练研究来评估功能价值。共涵盖六个MoE架构，包括OLMoE、Mixtral和DeepSeek等模型，构成39个因子单元的系统比较。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 六个MoE架构中专家子空间显著重叠，但实际路由对标记表示的预测仍优于匹配替代方案；(2) 在全部39个因子单元中，被选中的专家解释残差表示的能力始终超过最强的未选中竞争者，但实际前缀在所有情况下都缩小了这一优势，所有交互均为负且95%置信区间全部低于零；(3) 尽管存在几何上的收窄，添加后期专家在39个冻结路由比较中的24个中改善了下游预测，其余15个不具结论性；受控训练研究在三个随机种子中均支持Top-2优于Top-1。

Conclusion: 作者将发现的整体模式命名为"相干重叠"（coherent overlap）：路由从共享的几何邻域中选择与标记相关的专家，但有用的多专家计算仍然存在，无需不相交的线性覆盖。这揭示了几何相似性本身不能决定功能冗余或剪枝价值，分离这些数量对于理解MoE模型的路由决策和进行有效的模型压缩具有重要意义。

Abstract: Sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) language models route each token to multiple experts, suggesting a geometric account of their benefit: co-selected experts should contribute distinct representation directions. Existing evidence often conflates route coherence, candidate quality, and candidate-by-context interaction. We distinguish these quantities using an Expert Subspace Separation Index (ESSI), matched-route residuals, and a prefix-controlled $2\times2$ factorial; frozen-route interventions and a controlled Top-$k$ study assess functional value. Three paired contrasts organize the findings. First, across six MoE architectures, expert subspaces overlap substantially, yet actual routes explain token representations better than matched alternatives. Second, across the 39 factorial cells in OLMoE, Mixtral, and DeepSeek, the selected candidate explains more of the residual representation than the strongest unselected rival in every cell, yet the actual prefix narrows this advantage throughout: all interactions are negative, and every 95% confidence interval lies below zero. Third, this geometric narrowing does not imply functional redundancy: adding later experts improves next-token prediction in 24 of 39 frozen-route comparisons, while the other 15 estimates are inconclusive; a controlled training study also favors Top-2 over Top-1 in all three seeds. We call this joint pattern coherent overlap: routing selects token-relevant experts from a shared geometric neighborhood, while useful multi-expert computation persists without disjoint linear coverage. Separating these quantities clarifies why geometric similarity alone cannot determine redundancy or pruning value.

</details>


### [98] [Measuring Distortion in the Empty Regions of Dimensionality Reduction Scatterplots with the Gap Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28324)
*Jaume Ros,Alessio Arleo,Fernando Paulovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的二维投影质量度量指标——Gap Index (GI)，通过将投影空间分解为空三角形并与其高维对应物比较来量化空白区域的视觉失真，弥补了现有质量指标忽略布局空白区域失真的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的质量度量指标主要关注点与点之间的直接关系（如距离或邻域），而忽略了2D布局中空白区域的失真，但这些空白区域往往构成视觉上重要的特征，影响用户对投影结果的解读信心。

Method: 将投影空间分解为空三角形，将其与高维空间中的对应三角形进行比较以计算变形程度；每个三角形的变形可以聚合成单一标量值，或叠加在投影上以可视化局部失真模式。

Result: 与流行的质量度量指标相比，GI对具有高视觉影响的小型结构变形敏感；同时计算速度快且结果易于解释。

Conclusion: GI是一种快速、可解释且对视觉重要的小型结构变形敏感的新质量度量指标，弥补了现有指标在空白区域失真评估方面的空白。

Abstract: Quality metrics play a crucial role in the proper use of dimensionality reduction projections for visual analysis of high-dimensional data. They quantify the degree of distortion of a projection compared to the high-dimensional data and provide a reliable indication of how confident users can be in the structures they see in the resulting layouts. However, most popular metrics focus on capturing direct relationships between points (e.g., distances or neighborhoods) while neglecting distortions in empty areas of the layout, even though these often compose visually relevant features of a 2D layout. In this paper, we introduce the Gap Index (GI), a quality metric for 2D projections that captures visual distortion by measuring spatial distortion in empty areas of a projection. It does so by decomposing the space into empty triangles, which are then compared to their high-dimensional counterparts to compute the deformation. This per-triangle deformation can be aggregated into a single scalar value or overlaid on a projection to visualize regional distortion patterns. Results show that, contrary to popular quality metrics, the GI is sensitive to small structural deformations that have high visual impact. It is also fast to compute and interpretable.

</details>


### [99] [Encryption-Compatible Clustered Federated Learning via Distributed Expectation-Maximization over Metadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28338)
*Michael Ben Ali,Imen Megdiche,André Péninou,Olivier Teste*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FLAMECHE框架，将基于元数据的聚类联邦学习(CFL)重新形式化为分布式期望最大化(EM)过程，仅通过加法操作进行服务器端更新，从而兼容实际的安全联邦学习加密方案，解决了CFL中隐私保护、通信成本和计算效率三者之间无法兼顾的三难困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CFL方法面临隐私保护、通信成本和计算效率之间的权衡问题（即CFL三难困境）。基于元数据的聚类方法虽然通信和计算高效，但与标准的联邦学习隐私保护机制不兼容，需要在不牺牲效率的前提下实现隐私保护。

Method: 将基于元数据的CFL重新设计为分布式期望最大化(EM)过程，将服务器端更新限制为加法操作，从而兼容实用的安全联邦学习加密方案，同时保持聚类效率。

Result: 在多个数据集和多种异构场景下的大量实验表明，FLAMECHE提高了客户端模型的有效性，并实现了加密兼容的元数据聚类，改善了其在CFL三难困境中的定位。

Conclusion: FLAMECHE成功解决了CFL三难困境中的关键限制，使得基于元数据的聚类方法能够同时兼顾隐私保护、通信成本和计算效率三个维度，为聚类联邦学习提供了一种隐私友好且高效的新范式。

Abstract: Clustered Federated Learning (CFL) addresses data heterogeneity in federated settings by grouping clients with similar data distributions to enable effective training. Existing methods face a trade-off between privacy preservation, communication cost, and computational efficiency. We formalize this as the CFL trilemma, according to which improving two of these dimensions comes at the expense of the third. A prominent paradigm relies on metadata (i.e., low-dimensional representations of client datasets shared with the server) to enable communication- and computation-efficient clustering. However, such approaches are not compatible with standard FL privacy-preserving mechanisms. To address this limitation, we propose FLAMECHE, which reformulates metadata-based CFL as a distributed Expectation-Maximization (EM) procedure, restricting server updates to additive operations while preserving efficiency. This design enables compatibility with practical secure FL schemes. We conducted extensive experiments on multiple datasets under various heterogeneous scenarios. Results show that FLAMECHE improves the effectiveness of client models. It enables encryption-compatible metadata-based clustering, enhancing its positioning within the CFL trilemma.

</details>


### [100] [LEDGERMIND: Provenance-Constrained Multimodal Agentic Reasoning with a Structured Evidence Ledger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28374)
*Enjun Du,Hange Zhou,Chenxu Du,Siyi Liu,Zirong Chen,Ziyu Zheng,Yongqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LedgerMind框架，将多模态智能体轨迹建模为溯源约束的状态机，通过结构化证据账本、三层接地协议、自适应双路径调度器和事件触发验证修复引擎，解决最终答案准确率无法揭示推理过程忠实性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态问答智能体的评估仅依赖最终答案准确率，无法判断答案是否来自有根据的证据、语言先验还是错误抵消。这种聚合信号掩盖了中间推理不可靠、实体幻觉、过度推理和修复放大等系统性失败模式。

Method: 将智能体轨迹视为溯源约束状态机：工具输出归一化为结构化证据账本作为轨迹状态，推理和决策仅能引用活跃账本条目；在实体和数值层面检查接地性；修复以类型化状态转换实现，不能引入无工具溯源的内容。配套三层接地协议、按问题复杂度匹配推理深度的自适应双路径调度器，以及带形式化溯源非放大保证的事件触发验证修复引擎。

Result: 在多个多模态推理基准和不同骨干多模态大语言模型上的实验表明，LedgerMind同时提升了答案准确率和轨迹级忠实性，有效针对了四种最终准确率容易掩盖的失败模式：无支撑的中间推理、引用驱动的实体幻觉、简单查询上的过度推理以及修复阶段的放大问题。

Conclusion: 以溯源约束为核心的结构化证据管理框架能够在提升最终答案准确性的同时保证推理过程的可信度和透明度，为多模态智能体可靠评估提供了超越最终准确率的新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal agents for visual question answering increasingly operate as multi-step trajectories that interleave perception, retrieval, and reasoning, yet evaluation still largely reduces to final-answer accuracy. This aggregate signal cannot tell whether a correct answer was reached through grounded evidence, language priors, or accidental error cancellation. We propose to treat a multimodal agent trajectory as a provenance-constrained state machine: tool outputs are normalized into a Structured Evidence Ledger that serves as the trajectory state, downstream reasoning and decision claims may cite only active ledger entries, grounding is checked at the entity and numeric level, and repair is realized as typed state transitions that cannot introduce content without tool-produced provenance. We instantiate this design as LedgerMind (Provenance-Constrained Multimodal Agentic Reasoning with a Structured Evidence Ledger), augmented by a Three-Layer Grounding Protocol, an Adaptive Dual-Path Dispatcher that matches reasoning depth to question complexity, and an Event-Triggered Verification-and-Repair engine with a formal provenance non-amplification guarantee. We use LedgerMind to target four recurring failure patterns that final-answer accuracy tends to obscure: unsupported intermediate reasoning, citation-backed entity hallucination (Phantom Grounding), over-reasoning on simple queries, and repair-time amplification. Experiments across multiple multimodal reasoning benchmarks and backbone MLLMs show that LedgerMind improves both answer accuracy and trajectory-level faithfulness.

</details>


### [101] [Hierarchical Multilevel Monte Carlo for Order-Optimal Neural Actor-Critic in Average-Reward CMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28390)
*Ankur Naskar,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层多级蒙特卡洛（MLMC）神经评论家方法，解决了平均奖励约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）中神经评论家的偏差-成本权衡问题，并在此基础上开发了原始-对偶自然演员-评论家算法，首次实现了无限时域平均奖励CMDP下的一般策略参数化和神经评论家的阶最优收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管线性评论家的原始-对偶演员-评论家方法已被充分理解，但在平均奖励CMDP中将阶最优收敛保证扩展到神经评论家仍然是一个开放性难题。核心障碍在于神经评论家估计中存在根本性的偏差-成本权衡：在神经正切核（NTK）分析下，减少评论家偏差会大幅增加评论家优化成本，从而阻碍原始-对偶框架中的阶最优收敛。

Method: 引入分层多级蒙特卡洛（MLMC）神经评论家，在轨迹采样和评论家优化两个维度上同时进行去偏。该估计器以仅为对数级别的期望样本成本，获得与长评论家优化运行相当的偏差水平。在此基础上，构建了原始-对偶自然演员-评论家算法。

Result: 所提出的算法实现了阶为O(T^{-1/2})的最优性差距和约束违反量，建立了无限时域平均奖励CMDP中具有一般策略参数化和神经评论家的首个阶最优收敛保证，且无需知道底层混合时间。该结果即使在无约束设置下也是新颖的。

Conclusion: 本文通过MLMC技术有效突破了神经评论家估计的偏差-成本瓶颈，首次为平均奖励CMDP提供了一般策略参数化下的阶最优收敛保证，推动了安全强化学习中神经函数逼近的理论进展。

Abstract: Constrained Markov Decision Processes (CMDPs) provide a natural framework for reinforcement learning in safety-critical applications, where agents maximize long-term reward while satisfying long-term constraints. Although primal-dual actor-critic methods with linear critics are well understood, extending order-optimal convergence guarantees to neural critics in average-reward CMDPs has remained open. The main challenge is a fundamental bias-cost trade-off in neural critic estimation: under Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) analysis, reducing critic bias substantially increases critic optimization cost, preventing order-optimal convergence in the primal-dual framework. We resolve this bottleneck by introducing a hierarchical Multilevel Monte Carlo (MLMC) neural critic that performs debiasing simultaneously across trajectory sampling and critic optimization. The resulting estimator attains the bias of a long critic optimization run with only logarithmic expected sample cost. Building on this estimator, we develop a primal-dual Natural Actor-Critic algorithm that achieves both an optimality gap and a constraint violation of order $\tilde{O}(T^{-1/2})$. This establishes the first order-optimal convergence guarantees for infinite-horizon average-reward CMDPs with general policy parameterization and neural critics, while eliminating the need to know the underlying mixing time. Our results are novel even in the unconstrained setting.

</details>


### [102] [Why Are GUI Agents Correct but Late? Decode on the Decision-Time Critical Path, Tested with Pre-Compiled Policy Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28399)
*Zihan Dong,Rui Qian,Qishi Zhan,Dongshen Peng,Kaixin Li,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应预期策略树(AAPT)，通过在空闲屏幕期间预先构建有界条件策略树来消除计算机使用智能体在决策关键路径上的自回归解码延迟，将瞬态GUI事件处理成功率从0.50提升至0.79，且不产生错误动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体经常因瞬态GUI事件而失败——它们在相关窗口关闭后才产生正确动作。根本原因是昂贵的自回归解码处于决策时间的关键路径上，导致响应延迟。

Method: 在空闲屏幕时段，利用同一个冻结多模态模型构建有界条件策略树，包含可观察的守卫条件、预授权动作和分支特定截止时间。树的大小设计为覆盖模型自身的解码延迟。事件发生时，轻量级观察器将变化门控帧匹配到准备好的分支并立即执行对应动作，无需生成新文本。这种树将推理延迟从决策关键路径中移出。

Result: 在预注册配对试验中，AAPT将成功率从0.50提升至0.79（p=1.8×10^-3），且无错误动作。开环和预测-重规划基线因仍在执行期间解码而成功率均为零。消融实验揭示三个关键需求：快速观察器解码、有效树规划和准确分支路由。预注册的Oracle探测推翻了初始假设，指出分支路由是因果瓶颈。在独立通用多模态模型上126次配对试验中复现（p=4.9×10^-13）。外部基准测试中与反应式基线整体性能相当但优势互补。

Conclusion: AAPT在候选动作可预先枚举的场景中表现最佳，而反应式执行在动作无法预先枚举时仍具优势。这表明将解码延迟从决策关键路径中移出的策略树方法适用于可枚举动作空间，但方法选择应取决于任务的可预测性。

Abstract: Computer-use agents often fail on transient GUI events because they produce the correct action only after the relevant window has already closed. We identify the main cause as expensive autoregressive decoding on the decision-time critical path. We propose Adaptive Anticipatory Policy Trees (AAPT), which eliminates this delay without modifying the underlying model. During idle screen periods, the same frozen multimodal model constructs a bounded conditional policy tree with observable guards, pre-authorized actions, and branch-specific deadlines. The tree is sized to cover the model's own decoding latency. When an event occurs, a lightweight observer matches change-gated frames to a prepared branch and immediately executes the corresponding action without generating new text. In paired trials with pre-registered endpoints and exact McNemar tests, AAPT improves the success rate from 0.50 to 0.79 within a contested decision window ($p=1.8\times10^{-3}$), while producing no incorrect actions. Both open-loop and predict-and-replan baselines achieve zero success because they still decode during execution. A preparation-time sweep shows that the gain emerges where the latency-based tree-sizing rule predicts, and ablations reveal three key requirements: fast observer decoding, valid tree planning, and accurate branch routing. A pre-registered oracle probe rejects our initial hypothesis and instead points to branch routing as the causal bottleneck. We further reproduce the effect on an independent general-purpose multimodal model over 126 paired trials ($p=4.9\times10^{-13}$). On an external benchmark, AAPT matches the overall performance of a reactive baseline, although the two methods exhibit complementary strengths. Together, these results suggest that AAPT performs best when candidate actions can be enumerated in advance, whereas reactive execution remains stronger when they cannot.

</details>


### [103] [On-Policy and Off-Policy Learning for Large Action Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28408)
*Imad Aouali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了交互式系统中在大动作空间下的策略学习问题，涵盖在线学习（上下文老虎机）与离线学习两大范式，提出了多种结构化贝叶斯方法和悲观方法来解决探索效率低、数据覆盖稀疏、重要性权重高方差等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大动作空间的上下文老虎机问题中，在线与离线策略学习都面临严峻挑战：在线学习存在探索效率低下和数据覆盖稀疏的问题，离线学习则面临高方差的重要性权重、外推偏差以及困难的优化景观。这些问题严重制约了策略学习在大规模实际应用中的表现。

Method: 本文提出两类方法：(1) 在线学习方面，提出meTS（混合效应汤普森采样）和dTS（基于扩散启发先验的汤普森采样），利用结构化贝叶斯方法在动作间共享信息；(2) 离线学习方面，提出基于潜变量的sDM结构化直接方法，引入凹的、可高效优化的策略加权对数似然目标，并开发基于指数平滑和PAC-Bayesian界的可微悲观方法来控制正则化重要性采样估计器的偏差-方差权衡。

Result: 方法在理论上提供了依赖于有效动作数量的遗憾保证，并证明了在大动作空间中优化误差可能主导估计误差这一重要发现。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地解决了大动作空间下在线与离线策略学习的核心挑战，通过结构化先验、潜变量模型和悲观估计等技术，在理论上保证了性能，为大规模交互系统中的策略学习提供了系统性的解决方案。

Abstract: This thesis studies policy learning in interactive systems where an agent observes a context, selects an action from a very large set, and receives partial feedback. The main framework is contextual bandits, with two paradigms: on-policy learning, where the agent interacts sequentially with the environment and minimizes regret, and off-policy learning, where it learns from logged data collected by a logging policy. In large action spaces, both settings face major challenges: inefficient exploration, sparse data coverage, high-variance importance weights, extrapolation bias, and difficult optimization landscapes. The first part develops structured Bayesian methods for on-policy learning. We introduce meTS, a mixed-effect extension of Thompson sampling, and dTS, which leverages diffusion-inspired priors to model dependencies between actions. These methods share information across actions and yield regret guarantees depending on an effective number of actions. The second part addresses off-policy learning. We propose sDM, a structured direct method based on latent variables, show that optimization error can dominate estimation error in large action spaces, and introduce concave, efficiently optimizable policy-weighted log-likelihood objectives. Finally, we develop differentiable pessimistic methods based on exponential smoothing and PAC-Bayesian bounds to control the bias-variance trade-off of regularized importance-sampling estimators.

</details>


### [104] [QQWorld: Quantile-Quantile Matching for World Model Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28415)
*Zhoushun Yu,Xiaoyu Hu,Xiangyu Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QQWorld通过用量纲-量纲（QQ）匹配目标替代Epps-Pulley（EP）目标来改进潜世界模型的规划性能，解决了EP在尾部样本上校正梯度快速消失的问题，并引入跨批次QQ策略进一步优化排名池，在四个控制环境中显著提升了规划成功率、高斯对齐度和尾部厚度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型通过预测紧凑表示空间中的未来状态来实现高效规划，其性能严重依赖于学习到的潜分布质量。LeWorldModel（LeWM）虽然使用Epps-Pulley（EP）目标将潜变量正则化为各向同性高斯分布，但EP的校正梯度在孤立的尾部样本上会快速消失，导致重尾偏差得不到有效控制，从而限制了模型性能。

Method: 该方法提出了QQWorld，核心思想是用分位数-分位数（QQ）匹配目标替代EP目标，直接对齐投影后的潜样本与秩匹配的高斯分位数，从而在尾部区域也能保持有效的校正梯度。此外还开发了跨批次QQ（cross-batch QQ）策略，利用之前批次中分离的样本扩大有效排名池，并分析了该策略的偏差-方差权衡。

Result: 在四个控制环境中，QQWorld有效地提高了LeWM的平均规划成功率，同时始终取得更好的高斯对齐度和更薄的潜分布尾部，验证了该方法在改进潜世界模型性能方面的有效性。

Conclusion: QQWorld证明了QQ匹配目标比EP目标更适合潜世界模型的分布正则化，尤其在尾部处理上具有明显优势，跨批次策略为排名池的扩展提供了可行的方案，并通过实验验证了方法在规划和潜分布质量上的全面提升。

Abstract: Latent world models enable efficient planning by predicting future states in a compact representation space, but their performance depends critically on the quality of the learned latent distribution. LeWorldModel (LeWM) regularizes its latents toward an isotropic Gaussian using the Epps-Pulley (EP) objective. We show that the corrective gradients of EP rapidly vanish for isolated tail samples, leaving heavy-tailed deviations insufficiently controlled. To address this limitation, we propose QQWorld, which replaces EP with a quantile-quantile matching objective that directly aligns projected latent samples with rank-matched Gaussian quantiles, thereby maintaining effective corrective gradients in the tails. We further develop cross-batch QQ, which enlarges the effective ranking pool using detached samples from previous batches, and characterize its bias-variance trade-off. Across four control environments, QQWorld effectively improves the average planning success rate of LeWM, while consistently yielding better Gaussian alignment and thinner latent tails.

</details>


### [105] [QAdapt: A Noise-Adaptive Neural Pre-Decoding Framework for Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28422)
*Ran Miao,Rui Luo,Xiaohan Shan,Xiaoming Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QAdapt是一个噪声自适应的神经预解码框架，用于表面码量子纠错，通过捕获综合征数据的局部时空相关性并顺序适应噪声变化来提升逻辑错误率和后端解码延迟。在110种合成OOD噪声配置中一致降低逻辑错误率，在Google Willow基准数据上实现高达5.79%的逻辑错误率降低和9.32%的后端解码延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算不仅受物理噪声限制，还受经典解码器处理迅速生成综合征数据的延迟限制。硬件噪声强、异构且非平稳，且模拟到硬件的分布偏移会显著降低固定神经解码器的性能，亟需噪声自适应的解码框架。

Method: 提出QAdapt噪声自适应神经预解码框架，包含三个关键组件：捕获综合征数据中局部时空相关性；顺序适应不断变化的噪声条件同时缓解灾难性遗忘；将残余综合征转发给传统全局解码器。

Result: 在110种合成分布外噪声配置下一致降低逻辑错误率；在Google Willow基准数据上无需目标域微调即实现最多5.79%的逻辑错误率降低和9.32%的后端解码延迟降低。

Conclusion: QAdapt提供了一种实用且与解码器兼容的方法，可在不断演变的硬件噪声下提升量子纠错的鲁棒性和后端解码效率。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC) relies on quantum error correction to suppress physical errors and preserve logical information at scale. In practice, however, performance is constrained not only by physical noise but also by the latency of classical decoders processing rapidly generated syndrome data. This challenge is exacerbated by hardware noise that is strong, heterogeneous, and nonstationary, as well as by the simulation-to-hardware distribution shift that can substantially degrade fixed neural decoders. We present QAdapt, a noise-adaptive neural pre-decoding framework for surface-code quantum error correction. QAdapt captures local spatiotemporal correlations in syndrome data, sequentially adapts to evolving noise conditions while mitigating catastrophic forgetting, and forwards the residual syndrome to a conventional global decoder. Across 110 synthetic out-of-distribution noise configurations for rotated surface-code memory circuits, QAdapt consistently reduces the logical error rate relative to the neural pre-decoding baseline. On Google's Willow benchmark data, without target-domain fine-tuning, it achieves reductions of up to 5.79 percent in logical error rate and 9.32 percent in backend decoding latency on the residual syndrome. These results demonstrate that QAdapt provides a practical and decoder-compatible approach to improving the robustness and backend decoding efficiency of quantum error correction under evolving hardware noise.

</details>


### [106] [Kohn-Sham Spectral Embedding on Sparse Graphs at the Nishimori Temperature for Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28428)
*V. S. Usatyuk,D. A. Sapozhnikov,S. I. Egorov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kohn-Sham光谱嵌入（KSSE），一种基于物理启发的能量模型，将密集CNN分类器替换为在相关随机键Ising模型的Nishimori温度下评估的稀疏图光谱嵌入。通过在准循环LDPC图上构造Kohn-Sham哈密顿量，利用FFT在O(N log N + k²mode N)时间内求解D个独立通道谱问题，在ImageNet-1000上以约2124万参数达到88.93%的Top-1准确率，超越Swin-L并匹配ViT-H/14的性能，同时将模型规模缩减10-30倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统密集CNN分类器参数规模庞大、计算成本高，且缺乏可解释性。作者旨在利用凝聚态物理中的Kohn-Sham框架和稀疏图理论，构建一种更高效、参数更少且具有严格理论保证的图像分类模型，以解决深度学习模型在效率和可解释性方面的瓶颈。

Method: 1) 将预训练特征映射到准循环LDPC图上，构造正则化Laplacian作为Kohn-Sham哈密顿量；2) 利用环块上的FFT（借助Z/pZ的Pontryagin自对偶性）和低阶Rayleigh细化在O(N log N + k²mode N)时间内求解谱问题；3) 提出star-domain surgery优化图拓扑，通过构造边平移在码字周围产生局部凸性，同时将残余挫折度限制在ρ(B_γ)≤1+δ；4) 使用多尺度分形分析（D₂谱）和分形学习率景观来认证从粗糙区域到star-domain盆地的景观转变；5) 证明六个理论结果，包括广义Ihara-Bass恒等式、陷阱集特征值对应、加性通道可分离性等。

Result: 在ImageNet-1000上的转导协议中，使用冻结的EfficientNet-B4特征（D=1792），KSSE以约2124万参数达到88.93%的Top-1准确率，超越Swin-L（197M参数，86.4-87.3%）并匹配ViT-H/14（632M参数，88.0-89.5%）在标准归纳设置下的表现，模型规模分别缩减10倍和30倍。

Conclusion: KSSE证明基于物理启发的稀疏图光谱嵌入可以在极大减少参数量的同时达到甚至超越最先进的密集Transformer模型的分类性能，为高效、可解释的深度学习架构提供了新的范式，并建立了从统计物理（Ising模型）、图论（LDPC码）和密度泛函理论（Kohn-Sham方程）到机器学习分类器的跨学科桥梁。

Abstract: We introduce Kohn--Sham Spectral Embedding (KSSE), a physics-inspired energy-based model replacing dense CNN classifiers with a sparse-graph spectral embedding evaluated at the Nishimori temperature of an associated Random-Bond Ising Model. By mapping pre-trained features onto quasi-cyclic low-density parity-check graphs and constructing a regularized Laplacian acting as a Kohn--Sham Hamiltonian, we solve $D$ independent channel spectral problems in $\mathcal{O}(N\log N + k^2_{\text{mode}} N)$ time via FFT on circulant blocks (leveraging Pontryagin self-duality of $\mathbb{Z}/p\mathbb{Z}$) and low-order Rayleigh refinement. Graph topology is optimized using \emph{star-domain surgery}: rather than destroying information-carrying codewords by removing frustrated cycles, we construct edge shifts creating local convexity around codewords while bounding residual frustration to $ρ(B_γ)\leq 1+δ$. Multi-scale fractal analysis ($D_2$ spectrum) and fractal learning-rate landscape certifies a landscape transition from rough regimes ($D_2>3$) to star-domain basins ($D_2<1$), enabling Rayleigh refinement with $k_{\text{mode}}=5$ modes. We prove six theoretical results: a generalized Ihara--Bass identity linking belief propagation to the Laplacian; trapping-set eigenvalue correspondence; additive channel separability with an explicit exchange-correlation bound; a surgery theorem bounding frustration with attractor width $Ω(1/\sqrt{d_{\min}})$; a quasi-stationarity perturbation bound; and a fixed-point convergence theorem. In a transductive protocol on ImageNet-1000 with frozen EfficientNet-B4 features ($D=1792$), KSSE achieves \textbf{88.93\%} Top-1 accuracy using $\approx 21.24$M parameters, outperforming Swin-L (197M, 86.4--87.3\%) and matching ViT-H/14 (632M, 88.0--89.5\%) under standard inductive setups, while reducing model footprint by $10\times$ and $30\times$, respectively.

</details>


### [107] [Oracle-Budgeted Molecular Optimization with Short-Term Graph Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28437)
*Jiannan Yang,Veronika Thost,Xiang Ling,Tengfei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"短期图记忆"的即插即用模块，通过在线图神经网络代理对候选分子进行预筛选，在固定oracle预算下更明智地选择查询分子，从而提升分子优化性能且不增加额外计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分子优化中，oracle预算（可评估的分子数量）通常是有限的，因此决定评估哪些分子与决定生成哪些分子同样重要。现有方法在如何高效利用有限oracle预算方面存在不足。

Method: 提出短期图记忆模块，在生成器中引入在线图神经网络代理，对每轮生成的候选分子池进行预筛选，优先将有限的oracle预算分配给预测效用更高的分子。该模块保持原有生成器架构和更新规则不变，仅作为插件集成。

Result: 在标准分子优化基准上，将该模块应用于基于片段的生成器，平均top-10分数在所有oracle上均有提升且未落后于基线；在1000次调用的紧凑预算下，四个测试生成器均有增益。分析表明其收益与骨干的搜索广度和利用oracle反馈的效率相关。

Conclusion: 提供了一种简单有效的方法来更选择性地分配固定oracle预算，并为哪些生成器能从该策略中受益提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Molecular optimization is commonly performed under a limited oracle budget, which makes deciding what to evaluate as important as deciding what to generate. We introduce short-term graph memory, a plug-in module that preserves the generator architecture and native update rule while learning from previously evaluated molecules to prioritize subsequent oracle queries. The module maintains an online graph neural surrogate that pre-screens each round's candidate pool, so the fixed oracle budget is spent on molecules with higher predicted utility. Applied to a fragment-based generator on a standard molecular optimization benchmark, it improves the mean top-10 score at no extra oracle cost and never falls behind the base on any oracle; the gain extends to all four generators we tested at a tight budget of one thousand calls. We then analyze how surrogate-guided selection interacts with the exploration and exploitation behavior of different generators. Its benefit at larger budgets is consistent with two properties of the backbone: how broadly it searches, and how effectively its native search already exploits oracle feedback. We provide a simple way to spend a fixed oracle budget more selectively, and evidence on which generators benefit from it.

</details>


### [108] [Cybersecurity Detection Classification with Reasoning-enabled Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28460)
*Amol Khanna,Manu Nandan,Cristian Viorel Popa,Joan Pujol-Roig,Diana Bolocan,Laura Vasilie,Alexandru Apostu,Chase Helwig,Mihaela Gaman,Michael Brautbar,Edward Raff,Chase Midler,Sven Krasser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于链式思维（CoT）推理的安全告警分类系统，通过自动化提示优化、自训练和带可验证奖励的强化学习训练推理能力，并额外训练一个校准器来读取完整推理轨迹以估计判定正确的概率，在真实Windows终端告警数据上达到82.6%的测试准确率，在高置信度操作点下良性召回率提升43.0%、恶意召回率提升18.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心（SOC）面临严重的告警疲劳问题，检测数量远超人员日常处理能力。现有方法虽然使用大语言模型（LLM）直接输出分类标签，但缺乏对检测是否真正构成威胁的推理能力训练，导致自动化分类的可靠性和置信度估计不足。

Method: 在真实人工标注的Windows终端检测数据上训练一个CoT推理分类器，结合三种训练策略：自动化提示优化、自训练、以及带可验证奖励的强化学习。同时，由于CoT推理会降低标签token概率的可靠性，作者单独训练一个校准器，读取完整推理轨迹并估计判定正确的概率。

Result: 系统达到82.6%的测试准确率。在高置信度操作点下，相比直接标签LLM分类器，良性召回率提升43.0%，恶意召回率提升18.3%。实验还表明：未训练的置信度判断器会使高置信度召回率降至零，证明校准器训练的必要性；微调的30B模型显著优于前沿通用大模型，说明定向训练比单纯扩大模型规模更有效。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，结合CoT推理训练和专门的置信度校准器能够显著提升自动化告警分类的性能和可靠性。同时，针对特定领域进行定向微调比依赖更大规模的前沿通用模型更具实用价值，为SOC告警分诊提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: A major issue in Security Operations Centers (SOCs) is alert fatigue, as the number of detections reported is more than staff can triage in a given day. Prior work prompts or fine-tunes large language models (LLMs) to emit a triage label directly, but does not train them to reason about whether a detection is a genuine threat. We train a chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning-enabled triage classifier on real, human-labeled Windows endpoint detections by combining automated prompt optimization, self-training, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. We find that CoT reasoning also degrades the label-token probabilities that automated triage relies on, so we separately train a calibrator that reads the full reasoning trace and estimates the probability that the verdict is correct. Our system reaches 82.6% test accuracy and, at the high-confidence operating point that governs automated triage, improves benign recall by 43.0% and malicious recall by 18.3% over a direct-label LLM classifier. We further show that the trained calibrator is necessary - an untrained confidence judge collapses high-confidence recall to zero - and that a finetuned 30B model significantly outperforms frontier general-purpose models, motivating targeted training over scale.

</details>


### [109] [Stage-Replay Divergence Follows the KV Cache: Fixed-Prefix Precision Controls and Bidirectional Cache Transplantation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28495)
*Alexander Boesgaard Lorup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过阶段回放诊断方法，检验了在大语言模型推理阶段边界上，用一次性预填充重建中间token前缀是否能忠实替代保留的实时缓存。实验在Qwen2.5衍生系统上进行，发现BF16精度下重建与实时缓存存在部分分歧（166/200个后缀不同，准确率仅差1点），而FP32下无任何解码分歧。通过双向移植全部48层KV缓存，所有分歧轨迹均跟随缓存供体，证明边界KV缓存是发散轨迹的因果充分载体，数值精度则调节其行为表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阶段回放诊断技术假设从解码器状态恢复中间token前缀后，用全新预填充继续生成可以达到与原始实时缓存等价的效果。但这一假设在真实的推理阶段边界上是否成立缺乏严格审计，尤其是数值精度（BF16 vs FP32）和缓存构建方式（增量 vs 一次性预填充）对行为的影响未被系统验证。

Method: 设计匹配的200项对照实验：一侧保留实时缓存，另一侧对完全相同的整数token进行一次预填充，两侧放置完全相同的副本。在固定前缀2x2设计中交叉构建方式（保留缓存vs重新预填充）和精度（BF16 vs FP32）。同时采用固定前缀保持200个token状态不变，进行4组实验（构建方式×精度）。还设计了12/12行的逐步桥接实验，使token级增量缓存与保留缓存达到逐位精确；以及双向移植全部48层KV层，在选中集（24/24）和后续检查点的盲法复制集（43/43）上验证因果方向。最后通过全200保存账本审计复现所有保留轨迹和比较指纹。

Result: BF16下，副本精确但两种构建方式在166个后缀和20个正确性标签上存在分歧，准确率差异仅1个点（配对95% CI [-3.5, +5.5]）。BF16分歧可持续复现，而FP32下无解码分歧（95% Wilson上界1.88%）。逐步桥接使增量缓存和保留缓存在12/12行上逐位精确；保存账本审计复现所有轨迹。双向移植48层KV缓存后，所有测试的发散延续均跟随缓存供体：主检查点24/24，后续检查点盲法复制43/43。

Conclusion: 精确token回放可以重复但不一定能保持实时状态保真度。在测试的状态上，边界KV缓存是发散轨迹的因果充分载体，而数值精度（BF16 vs FP32）调节了这种因果性的行为表现。这意味着阶段回放诊断对缓存保真度的假设需要修订：即使token完全相同，重建路径也可能产生行为分歧，但这种分歧的因果根源在于KV缓存而非token序列本身。

Abstract: Stage-replay diagnostics reconstruct intermediate token prefixes and treat fresh-prefill continuation as continuation from the decoder state that originally reached the prefix. We audit that assumption at a whole reasoning-stage boundary in a Qwen2.5-derived system. A matched 200-item experiment compares retained live cache with one-shot prefill of identical integer tokens and places an exact replica on both sides. In BF16, replicas remain exact while the constructions differ on 166 suffixes and 20 correctness labels; the accuracy difference is only one point (paired 95% CI [-3.5, +5.5]). A fixed-prefix 2x2 holds all 200 token states constant while crossing construction and precision. The BF16 disagreements recur, whereas FP32 produces no decoded disagreement (95% Wilson upper bound 1.88%). A prospective bridge makes token-by-token incremental and retained live caches bit-exact on 12/12 rows; an all-200 saved-ledger audit reproduces every retained trajectory and comparison fingerprint. Bidirectional transplantation of all 48 key/value layers makes every tested divergent continuation follow its cache donor, both on a selected set at the primary checkpoint (24/24) and an outcome-blind replication at a later checkpoint (43/43). Exact-token replay can therefore be repeatable without preserving live-state fidelity. On the tested states, boundary K/V cache is a causally sufficient carrier of the divergent trajectory, while numerical precision moderates its behavioral expression.

</details>


### [110] [The Role of Causality in Algorithmic Recourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28497)
*Srikanth Avasarala,Varun Gupta,Shahin Jabbari,Saber Salehkaleybar,Juba Ziani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个因果可执行框架（causal performative framework）来形式化算法救济（algorithmic recourse）中的失败模式——即现有方法只关注翻转模型预测，而忽略了推荐改变是否真正提升个体资质或只是策略性博弈，导致部署后预测精度下降且模型重训后失效。作者通过结构因果模型建模救济行为的影响，刻画了可执行稳定解存在的条件及高效计算方法，并在半合成和真实信用数据集上验证了因果救济优于标准经验风险最小化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法救济方法仅以翻转模型预测为目标，未考虑推荐行动是否真实改善个体的资格条件，可能诱发策略性博弈行为，导致模型在重训后预测精度下降且救济策略失效。

Method: 提出因果可执行框架（causal performative framework），将救济行动通过结构因果模型建模，捕捉特征间交互及其对真实标签的影响；将由此产生的因果响应建模为非凸优化问题，并刻画可执行稳定解的存在条件及通过简单迭代动力学高效求解的条件。

Result: 理论分析表明，忽略因果结构的救济策略会引发大规模、错位的行为响应，而因果救济能收敛到稳定均衡并降低博弈激励。在半合成和真实信用数据集上的实验表明，该方法一致优于标准经验风险最小化，同时减少了因策略性行为引起的分布偏移所需的重复模型重训。

Conclusion: 因果可执行救济能够实现执行稳定（performatively stable）的均衡解，减少策略性博弈激励，提升模型长期有效性并降低重训成本，是比忽略因果结构的传统救济策略更可靠的方法。

Abstract: Algorithmic recourse aims to provide individuals with actionable changes to improve their predicted outcomes in high-stakes classification settings, such as loan and mortgage applications. However, most existing approaches focus only on flipping a model's prediction, without accounting for whether the recommended changes lead to genuine improvement in an individual's true qualifications or merely enable strategic gaming of the classifier. Consequently, deployed recourse policies can induce behavioral responses that degrade predictive accuracy and become ineffective after model retraining.
  In this work, we formalize this failure mode through a causal performative framework for recourse. We model how recourse actions propagate through a structural causal model, capturing interactions among features as well as their effect on the true label. These causal responses induce a non-convex optimization problem, even under standard convex losses. We characterize conditions under which performatively stable solutions exist and can be efficiently computed via simple iterative dynamics. Our analysis reveals that recourse policies that ignore causal structure can induce large, misaligned behavioral responses, whereas causal recourse leads to stable equilibria that reduce incentives for gaming. Experiments on both semi-synthetic and real credit datasets demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms standard empirical risk minimization while reducing the need for repeated model retraining to accommodate distribution shifts caused by strategic agent behavior.

</details>


### [111] [Same Graph Cross-Task Transfer in GNNs: Protocols and Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28525)
*Neelam Akula,Surbhi Kumar,Murat Kantarcioglu,Baris Coskunuzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了同一图结构上节点分类（NC）与链接预测（LP）之间的跨任务迁移问题，提出了一种无泄漏的评估协议，并发现NC→LP在同质图上持续有益，而LP→NC仅在特定结构主导场景下可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在使用同一图结构上的跨任务评估时，存在数据划分不兼容、观测图假设不一致和负采样规则不同等问题，导致关于NC-LP跨任务迁移的结论不可靠。

Method: 形式化定义了同图NC-LP迁移，提出无泄漏协议（固定节点和边划分、排除评估边的共享消息传递图、LP使用固定负样本），在GCN、GraphSAGE、GPS三种骨干网络上进行系统实验，并提出CoTask Score (CTS)指标来综合评估共享编码器下NC+LP的联合效用。

Result: 迁移具有强烈的方向性和可预测性：NC→LP在同质图上持续有益，而LP→NC在朴素表示复用下甚至可能降低精度；LP→NC仅在结构主导（LP容易且NC未饱和）场景中可靠为正；同质性等简单数据集统计量可指导机制选择并避免负迁移。

Conclusion: 跨任务迁移的方向性和数据集属性（尤其是同质性）可以预测迁移效果，有助于在实际应用中避免负迁移并选择合适的迁移策略。

Abstract: Many real-world graphs support multiple predictive tasks over the same underlying structure, creating an opportunity to reuse supervision across node classification (NC) and link prediction (LP). However, existing evaluations often rely on incompatible splits, observed-graph assumptions, and negative sampling rules, making conclusions about same-graph cross-task transfer unreliable. We formalize same-graph NC-LP transfer and propose a leakage-free protocol that fixes node and edge splits, uses a shared message-passing graph that excludes evaluated edges, and employs fixed negatives for LP. Across three backbones (GCN, GraphSAGE, GPS), we find that transfer is strongly directional and predictable: NC $\to$ LP is consistently beneficial on homophilic graphs, while LP $\to$ NC is fragile and can even degrade accuracy under naive representation reuse. LP $\to$ NC becomes reliably positive mainly in a structure-dominant regime where LP is easy but NC is unsaturated, suggesting that LP acts as structural pretraining. Finally, we introduce the CoTask Score (CTS) to summarize joint NC+LP utility when a shared encoder must serve both tasks, and show that simple dataset statistics, especially homophily, can guide mechanism choice and help avoid negative transfer.

</details>


### [112] [APO: Unsupervised Atomic Policy Optimization for 3D Structure Prediction of Atomic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28553)
*Shentong Mo,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出APO（原子策略优化），一个完全无监督的3D原子结构对齐框架，通过双奖励机制（潜在结构模态奖励和热力学稳定性奖励）消除对地面真值参考结构的依赖，在晶体和抗体结构预测上超越全监督基线，同时提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流匹配模型（如FlowDPO）在3D原子结构预测中依赖监督偏好学习与地面真值坐标对齐，但获取新颖晶体相或从头设计蛋白质的实验标签成本极高，形成数据稀缺场景下的结构建模瓶颈。

Method: APO将组相对策略优化（GRPO）适配到3D原子环境，提出双奖励机制：(1) 通过对样本相似性进行特征分解来强化策略的主导潜在结构模态；(2) 强制热力学稳定性。模型在采样组内通过识别物理上合理的构型实现自我纠正。

Result: 在晶体和抗体结构预测基准上，APO一致优于全监督基线，刷新了匹配率和结构保真度的最优水平；同时有效拉直概率路径，显著提升推理效率。

Conclusion: 内在物理一致性可以比有噪声的监督坐标匹配更好地指导结构对齐，为数据稀缺场景下的原子结构建模提供了一种无监督替代方案。

Abstract: Predicting the 3D structures of atomic systems is fundamental to advancing material science and drug discovery. While flow-matching models (, FlowDPO) have recently shown promise in this domain, their performance relies heavily on alignment with ground-truth coordinates via supervised preference learning. However, obtaining experimental labels for novel crystal phases or de novo proteins is prohibitively expensive, creating a bottleneck for structural modeling in data-scarce regimes. In this work, we propose (Atomic Policy Optimization), a fully unsupervised alignment framework that eliminates the need for ground-truth reference structures. APO adapts group-relative policy optimization to 3D atomic environments, utilizing a novel dual-reward mechanism: (i) a that reinforces the policy's dominant latent structural modes through eigen-decomposition of sample similarities, and (ii) a that enforces thermodynamic stability. Our framework enables the model to ``self-correct'' by identifying physically plausible configurations within sampled groups. Extensive benchmarks on crystal and antibody structure prediction demonstrate that APO consistently outperforms fully supervised baselines, achieving a new state-of-the-art in match rates and structural fidelity. Furthermore, we show that APO effectively straightens probability paths, significantly improving inference efficiency. Our results suggest that intrinsic physical consistency can serve as a superior guide for alignment compared to noisy, supervised coordinate matching.

</details>


### [113] [$β$-OPSD: Deriving with Policy Optimization, Training with Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28582)
*Jiawei Xu,Minghui Liu,Juzheng Zhang,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出β-OPSD，将vanilla自蒸馏扩展到可控正则化参数β的通用框架，通过几何插值参考策略与特权教师，利用logit混合实现高效蒸馏，在数学推理基准上超越vanilla OPSD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vanilla OPSD在实践中表现脆弱，需要大量工程调优才能可靠工作。作者发现其根本原因在于vanilla OPSD本质上只是更广泛策略优化家族中β=1的特例，KL惩罚权重被隐式固定，缺乏可控的正则化来平衡参考策略与教师指导之间的关系。

Method: 作者将β从隐式固定值转化为可控正则化参数，推导出β-OPSD的最优策略为参考策略与特权教师之间的几何插值。为避免直接RL优化的高成本和高方差，利用闭式解构造蒸馏目标，通过在token级别混合参考策略与教师的logits来高效实现不同β值对应的蒸馏目标。此外引入return-to-go信用分配以对齐token更新与序列级目标。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的实验表明，β-OPSD一致性地超越vanilla OPSD，提升了优化稳定性和下游推理性能。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一条从自蒸馏到策略优化的原则性路径，既保持OPSD的实践效率，又通过可控的β参数获得了更优的性能与稳定性。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is a promising approach to improve reasoning language models, but it remains brittle in practice: making it work reliably often requires substantial engineering effort. We identify a structural source of this difficulty: vanilla OPSD is precisely the $β=1$ member of a broader policy-optimization family, where $β$ weights the KL penalty anchoring the student to a reference policy. This equivalence turns $β$ from an implicit value fixed at one into a controllable regularization parameter, yielding a more general formulation that trades off proximity to a reference policy against privileged teacher guidance. We introduce $β$-OPSD and derive its optimal policy as a geometric interpolation between the reference policy and the privileged teacher. Directly optimizing this objective with reinforcement learning, however, would be costly and high-variance. Rather than optimize the RL objective directly, we turn its closed-form solution into a distillation target. Each value of $β$ selects a target along the reference-to-teacher path, which we implement efficiently by mixing their token-level logits. In this way, inexpensive distillation approximates the solution of expensive policy optimization. Return-to-go credit assignment further aligns token updates with the sequence-level objective while retaining the simplicity of OPSD. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that $β$-OPSD consistently outperforms vanilla OPSD, improving optimization stability and downstream reasoning performance. Our results provide a principled route from self-distillation to policy optimization and back without sacrificing the efficiency that makes OPSD practical.

</details>


### [114] [KAISEN: Reproducible Subgroup Fairness Auditing for Clinical Risk Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28608)
*Sparsh Roy,Samuel Girmachew,Nishita Chavan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KAISEN，一个五阶段审计流水线，用于检测临床风险模型在不同患者子群体中的错误率差异。通过在16个疾病任务、15个社会决定因素轴和3个预定交叉的合成基准上进行压力测试，发现四个关键问题：显著性跟踪与最小可检测效应相关；阈值优化可改善平等化赔率差异但Platt缩放不稳定；机制诊断在代理误设定下完全失效且无失败信号；CUSUM阈值跨队列不可迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床风险模型在整体上表现良好，但在不同患者子群体间错误率差异显著。虽然已有审计流水线被提出，但其组件很少经过压力测试，因此不清楚审计的哪些部分可以在什么条件下被信任。本文旨在对审计流水线进行系统性的、直至失败点的评估。

Method: 构建KAISEN五阶段审计流水线，包括子群体分层、差异测量、机制诊断、事后缓解和漂移监控。在合成基准上评估，包含16个疾病任务、15个来自健康人民2030的社会决定因素轴和3个预定交叉，所有结果具有已知的真实标签。

Result: 四项主要发现：(i)显著性计数与各轴的原始平等化赔率差异的秩相关为0.56，用最小可检测效应标准化后升至0.78；(ii)按组阈值优化在48/48次留出运行中降低EOD，但分组Platt缩放表现为抛硬币(19/48改进)，应报告方差而非平均值；(iii)机制诊断在144/144个受控案例中正确分类，但在代理误设定下48个模型驱动案例全部无法恢复且无失败信号；(iv)CUSUM失败和误报由队列实现而非疾病驱动，参考阈值下27个误报和8个移位缺失中有7个来自不同种子。

Conclusion: 审计流水线的各组件在不同条件下可靠性差异巨大。机制诊断在误设定下会静默失败，模型缓解方法的平均效果具有误导性，CUSUM阈值不可跨队列迁移。所有结果均为合成数据，不建立临床有效性，但代码和工件已公开以支持复现。

Abstract: Clinical risk models routinely achieve strong aggregate performance while producing materially different error rates across patient subgroups. Audit pipelines have been proposed to catch this, but their components are rarely stress-tested, so it is unclear which parts of an audit can be trusted and under what conditions. We present KAISEN, a five-phase audit pipeline covering subgroup stratification, disparity measurement, mechanism diagnostics, post-hoc mitigation, and drift monitoring, evaluated to the point of failure on a synthetic benchmark of 16 disease tasks, 15 social-determinant axes from Healthy People 2030, and three prespecified intersections. Four findings follow. (i) Significance tracks each axis's gap against its own minimum detectable effect: rank correlation between significance count and raw equalized-odds difference (EOD) across the 15 axes is rho = 0.56, rising to rho = 0.78 once EOD is standardized by that floor. (ii) Per-group threshold optimization reduces EOD in 48 of 48 held-out runs (paired delta = -0.285, 95% CI [-0.313, -0.252]), while group-wise Platt scaling -- the better calibrator -- behaves as a coin flip on EOD (19 of 48 runs improved, 95% CI [0.26, 0.55]) with mean effect near zero, so what an audit should report is the variance, not the average. (iii) The mechanism diagnostic classifies 144 of 144 controlled cases correctly but recovers none of 48 model-driven cases under proxy misspecification, with no signal that it failed. (iv) CUSUM failures and false alarms track cohort realization far more than disease: at the reference threshold, all 27 false alarms and 7 of 8 missed shifts come from different seeds (chi-squared p = 0.002), so a threshold tuned on one cohort fails to transfer. All results are synthetic with known ground truth and do not establish clinical validity. Code, artifacts, and scripts reproducing every number are released.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Digital Harf: A Clinically Integrated Multimodal AI System for Pervasive Arabic Speech and Language Therapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27212)
*Asif Azad,Mohammad Sadat Hossain,MD Sadik Hossain Shanto,Sabri Boughorbel,Abdulrhman Aljouie,Bdour Alwuqaysi,Yahya Bokhari,Ayah Othman Sindi,Ehsan Hoque*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Digital Harf，一个面向阿拉伯语国家自闭症谱系障碍儿童的多模态AI言语治疗平台。该系统集成了三个治疗模块，并通过Agentic Synthetic Data Engine（ASDE）自动生成符合文化和治疗标准的阿拉伯语治疗内容。13位持证言语病理学家的评估显示，ASDE生成内容的临床接受率达90.1%，文化及语言对齐评分较高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语国家的自闭症儿童在获得言语治疗方面面临多重障碍：专业治疗师短缺、服务覆盖范围有限（主要集中在城市）、缺乏符合文化背景的数字治疗材料。现有技术解决方案未能充分解决这些根本问题。

Method: 构建Digital Harf平台，包含三个治疗模块（语言治疗、言语清晰度、图片描述），采用统一工作流并随儿童表现自适应调整。引入Agentic Synthetic Data Engine（ASDE）自动生成文化相关的图片、提示和语言任务，由明确的治疗和文化标准引导。

Result: 13位持证言语病理学家的专家评估显示，ASDE生成内容的临床接受率为90.1%（无需人工筛选或策划），平台整体的文化和语言对齐获得高度评价。

Conclusion: Digital Harf证明AI驱动的治疗系统可以从零开始为代表性不足的语言环境构建，将文化根基作为核心基础设施而非事后适配，为面向少数语言群体的AI治疗系统树立了范式。

Abstract: Children with Autism Spectrum Disorder in Arabic-speaking countries face compounded barriers to effective speech and language therapy: a shortage of qualified specialists, limited service reach beyond urban centers, and a near-total absence of culturally grounded digital therapy materials. We present Digital Harf, a pervasive, multimodal AI platform that extends clinician-led speech and language therapy into the home. The system integrates three therapeutic modules - language therapy, speech intelligibility, and picture description - within a unified workflow that adapts to each child's performance over time. To address the critical shortage of Arabic therapy content, we introduce an Agentic Synthetic Data Engine (ASDE) that automatically generates culturally relevant images, prompts, and language tasks guided by explicit therapeutic and cultural criteria. Expert evaluation with 13 licensed Speech-Language Pathologists yielded a 90.1% clinical acceptance rate for ASDE-generated content without any manual curation or selection, and strong ratings for cultural and linguistic alignment across the full platform. Digital Harf demonstrates that AI-driven therapeutic systems can be built from the ground up for underrepresented linguistic settings, treating cultural grounding as core infrastructure rather than an adaptation afterthought.

</details>


### [116] [Evaluating the Vergence-Accommodation Conflict in Gaze-Based 3D Target Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27369)
*Mohammad Raihanul Bashar,Mohammadreza Amini,Aunnoy K Mutasim,Mayra Donaji Barrera Machuca,Wolfgang Stuerzlinger,Anil Ufuk Batmaz*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了头戴式显示器（HMD）中辐辏调节冲突（VAC）对基于注视的3D目标选择性能的影响。结果表明视觉深度增加时，注视选择的用户性能显著下降，且变焦Fitts定律模型比线性模型更能捕捉这种性能变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HMD在虚拟环境中支持基于注视的目标选择，但存在辐辏调节冲突（VAC），该冲突直接影响眼球运动行为，可能对注视选择性能产生负面影响，此前缺乏相关研究。

Method: 在不同深度条件下进行基于注视的3D目标选择实验，比较VAC对选择性能的影响，并使用变焦Fitts定律模型与线性模型进行对比拟合分析。

Result: 随着视觉深度增加，基于注视的目标选择性能显著下降；变焦Fitts定律模型相比线性模型能更好地捕捉这种性能变化。

Conclusion: 注视式指向确实受到VAC的负面影响，在设计3D环境中的注视交互时需要考虑深度相关的因素。

Abstract: State-of-the-art head-mounted displays (HMDs) enable gaze-based selection in virtual environments. Yet, these HMDs suffer from the vergence-accommodation conflict (VAC), which is known to affect interaction performance. The VAC might influence gaze-based selection performance because it directly affects eye-movement behavior. Thus, in this paper, we investigate how the VAC influences gaze-based 3D target selection across varying depth conditions. Our results show that as the (visual) depth increases, user performance significantly decreases with gaze-based selection. Moreover, a previously suggested Variation in Diopter Fitts' law model captured this performance change better relative to a linear model. These findings provide evidence that gaze-based pointing is negatively affected by the VAC and highlight the importance of accounting for depth-dependent factors when designing gaze-based interaction in 3D environments.

</details>


### [117] [DP-LENS: A Density-Aware Polyfocal Lens with Topology-Driven Auto-Routing for Occlusion Management in Immersive 3D Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27697)
*Nieyu Cao,Xian Wang,Lik-Hang Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出DP-LENS，一种密度感知的多焦点鱼眼透镜系统，结合拓扑驱动的自动路由和基于LLM的语音辅助目标选择，用于在高度遮挡的3D数据中进行低认知负荷的探索。两项用户研究（共34名参与者）表明该系统显著降低了认知负荷、减少了任务完成时间，并获得了更高的用户偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沉浸式环境（如VR）中探索复杂3D数据集时，数据往往被严重遮挡，且探索过程会带来高认知负荷，需要一种能够降低认知负担并提高探索效率的交互方法。

Method: 提出DP-LENS密度感知多焦点鱼眼透镜，通过几何变形和3D透视技术保留外围上下文；集成大语言模型（LLM）作为基于语音的辅助目标选择工具，触发拓扑驱动的自动路由算法，实现免手动宏观导航。

Result: 研究一（N=18）：与行业标准基线（World-in-Miniature和体积切片）相比，DP-LENS显著降低了认知负荷、减少了完成时间并提升了用户偏好。研究二（N=16）：自动路由系统相比纯手动DP-LENS进一步提升了任务效率、降低认知负荷并获得更高偏好，同时部分解耦了探索效率与数据物理尺寸的关系，减轻了身体疲劳。

Conclusion: DP-LENS系统为3D可视分析提供了更低认知负荷和更高空间可扩展性的交互范式，研究结论可为未来3D可视分析系统中低疲劳、空间可扩展交互的设计提供指导。

Abstract: Immersive environments, e.g., virtual reality (VR), offer a unique approach to exploring complex 3D datasets, where data is often heavily occluded and exploration incurs a high cognitive load. We propose DP-LENS, a density-aware polyfocal fisheye lens equipped with topology-driven auto-routing. While preserving peripheral context through geometric deformation and 3D perspective techniques, it enables users to explore 3D data with a lower cognitive load. To facilitate hands-free macro-navigation, we integrate a Large Language Model (LLM) to serve as a supplementary voice-based target selection tool that initiates the auto-routing algorithm. Two user studies with 34 participants investigate the potential benefits of this system. Our first study (N=18) compared the manual DP-LENS against two industry-standard baselines (i.e., World-in-Miniature and volumetric slicing) in heavily occluded 3D datasets. The results show that DP-LENS significantly reduced cognitive load, decreased completion time, and improved user preference. The second study (N=16) compared the topology-driven auto-routing system (initiated via voice commands) with a fully manual DP-LENS. The results show that the auto-routing system improved task efficiency, further reduced cognitive load, and garnered higher user preference. Furthermore, the auto-routing partially decoupled exploration efficiency from the physical dimensions of the data and mitigated physical fatigue to some extent. Based on the findings, we proposed design implications to inform the development of more spatially scalable and low-fatigue interactions for future 3D visual analytics systems.

</details>


### [118] [Creative Task Cards for Reflection, Self-Efficiency and Self-Regulation in CS1 Introductory Programming: Initial Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27863)
*Corey Ford,Yinmiao Li,Rosa Van Koningsbruggen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文针对编程入门课程中学生的情感挑战（如兴趣低下和自我效能感不足），提出了创意任务卡片来支持学生的反思、自我效能感与自我调节。29名学生使用卡片完成模拟编码作业并参与调查和焦点小组，初步定性结果显示学生能从休息、绘画反思和分步推进中获益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机科学入门课程的学生常面临缺乏持续兴趣和自信不足等情感挑战，但课程通常只教授学科性策略（如调试策略），并未教授能帮助学生克服这些情感挑战的元认知或自我调节策略。

Method: 设计创意任务卡片以支持学生在编程作业中的反思、自我效能感和自我调节；29名学生使用卡片完成课堂内的模拟编码作业，随后参与问卷调查和焦点小组进行定性评估。

Result: 初步定性见解表明学生：（1）在离开教室与同伴反思休息中发现了价值；（2）能通过绘画更好地反思认知过载的感受；（3）在将工作分解为更小步骤后感到更有能力推进任务。

Conclusion: 创意任务卡片作为支持编程学习者情感与元认知发展的工具具有潜力，初步证据显示其有助于学生反思、增强自我效能感和促进自我调节。

Abstract: Computer Science students in introductory programming courses struggle with emotional challenges such as low levels of sustained interest and a lack of self-belief. However, students are typically introduced to disciplinary strategies (such as debugging strategies) and not the metacognitive nor self-regulation strategies that could help them overcome such emotional challenges. This paper addresses this issue by introducing creative task cards designed to support students' reflection, self-efficacy, and self-regulation in programming assignments. To evaluate the cards, twenty-nine students used them to complete a mock coding assignment in-class and participated in surveys and focus groups. Our initial qualitative insights suggest that students: found value in taking breaks, particularly when they could leave the classroom to reflect with peers; could use drawing to better reflect on feelings of cognitive overload; and felt more capable progressing with their work once breaking it into smaller steps.

</details>


### [119] [VizPilot: Automated Onboarding for SVG-based Composite Visualizations using Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27938)
*Nishaanthini Gnanavel,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: VizPilot是一个自动化可视化引导系统，通过多模态大语言模型逆向解析复合可视化结构，从原始可视化制品中直接生成交互式引导体验，无需大量人工编写工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合可视化虽然能有效表示复杂数据集，但其复合设计往往给新手用户带来较高的初始认知负担。现有的可视化引导方法依赖于特定平台、需要大量人工编写工作，且难以处理复合可视化的结构复杂性，限制了其通用性。

Method: VizPilot包含两个模块：复合可视化分析器和引导界面。分析器利用多模态大语言模型，采用两阶段流水线将可视化分解为视觉组件、提取结构化解释，并映射到精确的SVG元素以实现可靠的交互高亮。系统以浏览器扩展形式实现，只需开发人员提供简短的可视化描述和可选的交互源代码，即可自动生成引导内容。引导界面支持引导式叙事滚动叙事和自由探索两种模式。

Result: 通过不同输入模态的对比分析、展示降低编写工作量的使用场景以及评估认知负荷影响的用户研究，结果表明VizPilot能有效自动化引导体验的编写，同时提升复合可视化的可用性和可访问性。

Conclusion: VizPilot验证了利用多模态大语言模型自动化复合可视化引导的可行性和有效性，为降低可视化认知门槛提供了新的解决方案，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Composite visualizations integrate multiple visualizations to represent complex datasets effectively, but their intrinsic composite designs often impose a high initial cognitive load on novice users. Existing visualization onboarding approaches are typically platform-dependent, require substantial manual authoring effort, and struggle with the structural complexity of composite visualizations, limiting their general applicability. We present VizPilot, an automated visualization onboarding approach that reverse-engineers composite visualization structure to generate interactive onboarding experiences directly from raw visualization artifacts. VizPilot consists of two modules: a Composite Visualization Analyzer and an Onboarding Interface. Leveraging Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), the Analyzer employs a two-stage pipeline that decomposes a visualization into visual components, extracts structured explanations, and maps them to precise SVG elements for reliable highlighting and interaction. Implemented as a browser extension, VizPilot requires only a brief visualization description and optional interaction source code from the visualization developer to automatically generate onboarding content. The Onboarding Interface supports both guided narrative scrollytelling and free exploration, enabling users to learn visualization components progressively or on demand. We evaluate VizPilot through a comparative analysis of different input modalities, a usage scenario demonstrating reduced authoring effort, and a user study assessing its impact on users' cognitive load. The results demonstrate that VizPilot effectively automates the authoring of onboarding experiences while improving the usability and accessibility of composite visualizations.

</details>


### [120] [Investigating Effective Uncertainty Visualizations for Ordinal Crowdsourced Data of Crowding Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28072)
*Bea Alexis Arcega,Annika Dominique S. Campos,Kathleen Therese Cruz,Aaron Ace Toledo,Briane Paul V. Samson*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了如何在通勤场景中可视化人群拥挤度的不确定性（基于众包序数数据），通过在线实验评估了多种不确定性可视化方法，发现聚类可视化最能降低认知负荷并提升用户信心与信任，而气泡树图在判断拥挤等级时准确率最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通勤者在铁路系统中常遭遇拥挤，而拥挤程度随时间段变化产生不确定性，使人难以规划行程。虽然众包是收集本地化数据的有效方法，但众包信息的不确定性给决策带来挑战，而目前缺乏关于如何有效可视化拥挤度不确定性以支持通勤决策的研究，尤其是针对众包序数数据。

Method: 研究者设计了多种不同的不确定性可视化方案，用于呈现众包拥挤数据的变异性和可靠性，并通过在线用户研究进行评估，比较各可视化方法在认知负荷、用户信心、信任度和判断准确率等方面的表现。

Result: 研究发现聚类可视化在降低认知负荷、最大化用户信心和信任方面表现最佳；而气泡树图在帮助参与者准确判断拥挤等级方面准确率更高。

Conclusion: 不同不确定性可视化方法各有优势：聚类可视化适合提升用户体验和信任，气泡树图适合提高判断准确性。研究为众包序数数据的不确定性可视化设计提供了实证依据，有助于支持通勤者的出行决策。

Abstract: Commuters often encounter crowding in railway systems, particularly in queues where passenger density varies throughout the day. This introduces uncertainty in crowdedness, making it difficult for individuals to anticipate conditions and plan their trips effectively. Crowdsourcing has been a valuable method for collecting localized user data. But the unpredictability of crowds and the uncertainty of crowdsourced information pose new challenges for decision-making. However, we know little about how to effectively visualize uncertainty in crowdedness to support informed commuting decisions, particularly when using crowdsourced ordinal data. Here, we investigated different uncertainty visualizations and their effectiveness in representing the variability and reliability of crowdsourced crowding data. They were evaluated through an online study, and we found that cluster visualization is best suited to reduce cognitive load while maximizing user confidence and trust. On the other hand, participants showed higher accuracy in determining crowd levels when using bubble treemaps.

</details>


### [121] [A Mathematical Framework for Reading the Autopsias' Meta - Compositional System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28155)
*Patricio F. Calatayud,Pablo Padilla Longoria,Álvaro Martínez Ramírez*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文以Autopsias元作曲系统为案例，论证数学工具（特别是拓扑学概念）可以解决动态音乐记谱（cynetic musicography）的可读性问题，从而为作曲家与演奏者之间构建新的正交规则集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去20年出现的计算机音乐写作形式（如动态乐谱、算法处理、电影视角等）虽然利用数字操作能力创造了动态音乐记谱，但普遍存在可读性问题，目前缺乏系统性解决方案。

Method: 以Autopsias元作曲系统为研究案例，通过建立静态乐谱与动态计算再演绎之间的桥梁，引入基础拓扑学概念构建数学模型，并多次观察该作品的写作与演奏过程，进行数学视角分析。

Result: Autopsias的多次演奏表明，数学工具（如音级集合）有助于理解音乐信息及其演奏；同时证明现有正交规则集不足，需要扩充以改善作曲家与演奏者之间的对话。

Conclusion: 数学方法能够为动态音乐记谱的可读性提供分析框架，是构建适用于动态音乐记谱演奏的新型记谱工具的必要起点。

Abstract: Background. New forms of music writing using computers have arisen in the past 20 years. Most of them use the capacities of digital manipulation of data like animation, algorithmic processing, cinematic view, and much more. These scores use dynamic musicography, and all of them share a problem. They have readability problems. We will argue that this problem can be addressed by mathematical tools. Aims. Take the Autopsias [Autopsies] meta-compositional system as a study case for starting the construction of a mathematical framework that can overview the readability for cynetic musicography. The Autopsias system is the process of transforming a musical score dynamically. Methodology. We will start to build a mathematical framework by taking a group of basic topological concepts, and applying them after a bridge between a printed score and a dynamic computational re-appropriation of it has taken place. We will observe the writing and the performing of the several performances of the Autopsias composition, and analyze them from a mathematical perspective, in order to get the ability to start constructing a new set of orthographical tools for the performance of dynamic musicography. Main Contribution. The performances of Autopsias show that mathematics, as with pitch-class sets, can be helpful for understanding music information, as well as its performance. Also show the necessity of augmenting the set of orthographic rules, in order to improve the dialog between composers and performers.

</details>


### [122] [Student Perceptions and Preferences Regarding AI-Generated Instructional Videos in Computing Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28203)
*Esse Ciego,Shubbhi Taneja,Wilson Wong,Amanpreet Kapoor*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究调查了170名美国高校计算机专业学生对AI生成教学视频的感知。学生认为AI视频质量高、准确且可用，近半数无法辨别是否为AI生成，但对AI视频在课堂中的广泛采用持保留态度，倾向将其用于简单、补充性和视觉化的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在CS教育研究中主要聚焦于文本工具，但AI视频生成技术的快速发展使教师能够高效、低成本地制作个性化教学视频。了解学生对AI生成视频的感知，有助于CS教师判断何时以及如何使用这些视频。

Method: 采用描述性后测调查研究方法，在美國两所高校招募170名计算机专业学生，观看三个使用Knowlify制作的3分钟Markdown标记语言AI视频，随后完成关于视频感知及AI视频教育应用看法的问卷调查。

Result: 学生认为Markdown AI视频质量高、准确且可用，近半数学生无法判断视频是否为AI生成。但学生对AI视频在课堂中的广泛采用表现出有限的接受度，更倾向于将其用于简单、补充性和视觉化的场景，同时对低质量或不准确内容、师生互动减少以及教育价值下降表达了担忧。

Conclusion: AI生成视频在质量上已能满足教学需求，但学生的接受度存在矛盾：认可具体视频的质量，却对广泛采用持保留态度。CS教育者应有选择性地将AI视频用于合适的教学场景，而非全面替代传统教学方式。

Abstract: Students differ in how they prefer to engage with learning resources, with some favoring textual materials and others visual or video-based content. Recent advances in generative AI have led CS education research to focus on text-based AI tools for developing learning resources. However, advances in AI video models and the rapid proliferation of AI video generation tools have made it possible for instructors to create high-quality personalized educational videos efficiently and cost-effectively. Understanding students' perceptions of AI-generated videos is thus critical for helping CS instructors know when and how to use them purposefully. To address this gap, we conducted a descriptive post-test survey study in which 170 computing students at two U.S. institutions watched three 3-minute AI videos on the Markdown markup language created with Knowlify. Students then completed a survey about their perceptions of the Markdown videos and their broader views on the use of AI-generated videos in education. Students rated the Markdown videos as high-quality, accurate, and usable, with nearly half unable to determine whether the videos were AI-generated. At the same time, students expressed limited comfort with the widespread adoption of AI videos in the classroom. They preferred AI videos for simple, supplemental, and visual use cases, while expressing concerns about lower-quality or inaccurate content, reduced instructor interaction, and diminished educational value.

</details>


### [123] [Toward Annotation-Efficient Continuous Emotion Arousal Quantification via Group-Level EEG Dynamic Neural Synchrony](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28204)
*Guandong Pan,Yaqian Yang,Shi Chen,Yi Zheng,Yi Zhen,Hongwei Zheng,Shaoting Tang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出利用群体级脑电动态神经同步（DNS）作为连续情绪唤醒度量化的无标注信号，通过滑动窗口CorrCA在4个数据集（142名受试者，207+小时）上验证，发现DNS与唤醒度一阶导数相关性更强，并系统表征了窗口长度、滞后和特征选择等关键参数的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续情绪唤醒度的量化受限于耗时耗力的人工标注，亟需一种无需逐受试者手动标注的群体级信号来实现高效的连续情绪量化。

Method: 使用Correlated Component Analysis（CorrCA）结合滑动窗口计算，在4个EEG数据集（142名受试者、207小时以上）上系统评估群体级DNS作为情绪唤醒动态标记的有效性，并进行受试者分割复制和块置换检验。

Result: 三个关键发现：（1）DNS具有显著的情绪信息，正性情绪引发更高的同步性（所有p<0.003）；（2）DNS与唤醒度一阶导数的相关性高于原始唤醒值，表明神经同步捕捉的是情绪变化速率而非静态强度；（3）首次系统表征了DNS-唤醒耦合对方法论选择的依赖：中等窗口（10-30秒）、正滞后（0-10步）和主导CorrCA分量的一阶差分特征产生最强耦合。

Conclusion: DNS经实证验证可作为有效的群体级情绪唤醒标记，为无需人工标注的高效连续情绪唤醒量化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Continuous emotional arousal quantification remains bottlenecked by time-consuming and labor-intensive manual annotation. This work investigates group-level EEG dynamic neural synchrony (DNS) as a principled signal for continuous arousal quantification that bypasses per-subject manual labeling. Using Correlated Component Analysis (CorrCA) with sliding-window computation across four EEG datasets spanning 142 subjects and over 207 hours, we systematically evaluate DNS as a group-level marker for emotional arousal dynamics. Three key findings emerge. First, DNS exhibits significant emotion information from valence-dependent differences (all p<0.003), with positive emotions eliciting higher synchrony. Second, DNS correlates more strongly with the first-order derivative of arousal than with raw arousal values, revealing that neural synchrony captures the rate of emotional change rather than static intensity. Third, we provide the first systematic characterization of how DNS-arousal coupling depends on key methodological choices, finding that moderate windows (10-30 s), positive lags (0-10 steps), and First-order Difference feature of EEG from the dominant CorrCA component yield consistently strong coupling. Subject-split replication and block permutation tests confirm these associations are not statistical artifacts. Our findings establish DNS as an empirically validated group-level marker toward annotation-efficient continuous emotional arousal quantification.

</details>


### [124] [Identifying a Level-up Pathway for AI-assisted Counterspeech through Elaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28239)
*Han Li,Inhwan Bae,Natalie Bazarova,Drew Margolin*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过随机对照实验评估了三种生成式AI辅助的反疫苗言论反驳写作系统（协作写作vs.重写、引导vs.非引导），发现AI辅助写作能提升用户感知的反驳有效性，同时保留真实自我表达，且感知有效性是用户愿意公开发表反言论的最强预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社区驱动的反言论（counterspeech）是回应社交媒体上疫苗怀疑内容的有前景方式，但普通用户难以写出有效反驳，限制了其参与意愿和能力。研究旨在探索AI能否帮助普通用户创作既有效又真实的反驳内容。

Method: 设计并评估三种生成式AI辅助反言论写作系统，按辅助阶段（协作写作vs.重写）和模式（引导vs.非引导）区分。在随机对照试验中，社交媒体用户分别对统计型和叙事型疫苗怀疑内容撰写反言论，比较不同AI支持形式的效果及作用机制。

Result: AI辅助写作显著提升了感知的反驳有效性，同时保留了真实的自我表达。感知有效性是最强的公开反言论意愿预测因子。AI的主要益处在于促进更详尽的写作，产出的信息更具信息量、分析性和词汇复杂度。

Conclusion: 研究表明AI辅助的社区驱动反言论存在"升级路径"，能够培养更有效、更有动力的反言论者，为公共话语质量提升做出贡献。

Abstract: Given the profound societal impact of vaccine-skeptical content on social media, community-driven counterspeech has emerged as a promising participatory response to contest and curb such objectionable content. Yet crafting effective counterspeech remains challenging for ordinary users, limiting their willingness and ability to engage constructively. We designed and evaluated three generative AI-assisted counterspeech writing systems that vary by assistance stage (co-writing vs. re-writing) and mode (guided vs. unguided) to support lay users' responses to vaccine-skeptical content. We ask whether AI can help users craft counterspeech perceived as both effective and authentic, which forms of AI support work best, and through what mechanisms. In a randomized controlled trial with social media users, participants wrote counterspeech responses to both statistical and narrative vaccine-skeptical content. Across evidence types, AI-assisted writing increased perceived counterspeech effectiveness while largely preserving authentic self-expression, and perceived effectiveness was the strongest predictor of willingness to counterspeak publicly. AI's primary benefit was facilitating more elaborate writing, producing messages that were more informative, analytical, and lexically sophisticated. These findings suggest a level-up pathway for AI-assisted, community-driven counterspeech, which helps cultivate more effective and motivated counterspeakers, contributing to higher-quality public discourse on pressing societal issues.

</details>


### [125] [Effects of Auditory Information for People With Visual Impairments in Highly Automated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28544)
*Mark Colley,Tobias Aescht,Omid Rajabi,Max Rädler,Pascal Jansen,Enrico Rukzio*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究为全自动驾驶汽车内的视障用户设计了视觉与听觉信息沟通概念，并通过在线视频研究（N=35，其中12名视障参与者）比较了三种听觉信息层级。结果显示，增加信息量显著提升信任和用户体验，感知安全性也有相应改善，但中等信息量已接近信息饱和点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶汽车有望提升所有人的出行便利性，但当前的信息可视化设计研究并未充分关注视障用户的可访问性需求，存在研究空白。

Method: 设计并实现了面向视障用户的视觉与听觉信息沟通概念；开展在线视频研究（N=35，含12名视障参与者），比较低、中、高三种听觉信息层级对用户体验的影响。

Result: 增加信息量显著提升了信任和用户体验；感知安全性也有改善但效果较弱；中等信息量已达到潜在的信息饱和点。

Conclusion: 研究为自动驾驶汽车的可访问性改进提供了指导，帮助设计者选择合适的听觉信息沟通水平，以服务视障用户群体。

Abstract: Automated vehicles promise to improve accessibility and access to personal mobility for everyone. However, their design and current research trends in visualizing relevant information do not reflect this commitment to accessibility for users with visual impairments. Therefore, we designed and implemented a visual and auditory communication concept for people with visual impairments seated inside fully automated vehicles. Furthermore, in an online video-based study (N=35, 12 with visual impairments), we compared three levels of auditory information communication: low (safety-relevant information only), medium (additionally including vehicle control and route updates), and high (additionally including sightseeing and destination information). Results showed that trust and user experience significantly improved with additional information, with a corresponding, albeit less robust, effect on perceived safety. However, they also revealed that a potential information saturation was reached with medium information. Our work helps to improve the accessibility of automated vehicles by guiding designers towards adequate information communication.

</details>


### [126] [Multi-Session User Experience Assessments of Computationally Optimized Automated Vehicle Functionality Visualizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28552)
*Mark Colley,Pascal Jansen,Svenja Krauss,Enrico Rukzio*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出使用多目标贝叶斯优化方法来优化自动驾驶汽车中可视化界面的设计参数，旨在同时提升乘客信任度和感知安全性，并最小化认知负荷。通过在线研究（N=74）验证，该方法能有效识别出改善信任、安全和可预测性的设计参数组合，同时使设计过程更高效和可扩展，但也揭示了计算优化方法在处理主观测量指标时的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自动驾驶汽车对提高其接受度至关重要。现有的可视化方法虽然众多，但逐一进行实证评估耗时、昂贵且限制了可探索的设计参数空间，因此需要更高效的优化方法来指导可视化设计。

Method: 采用多目标贝叶斯优化方法对自动驾驶汽车中的可视化设计进行迭代优化，在多次会话中同时优化乘客信任度、感知安全性以及认知负荷三个目标，并通过在线研究（N=74）进行验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能够有效识别出同时改善乘客信任、安全感和可预测性的可视化设计参数组合，并且使设计过程更加高效和可扩展。

Conclusion: 多目标贝叶斯优化为自动驾驶可视化设计提供了一种高效可扩展的替代方案，但在处理主观测量指标时仍存在局限性，需要进一步讨论和改进。

Abstract: Understanding automated vehicles (AVs) is crucial to improving their acceptance. Numerous approaches to visualizing relevant traffic information to passengers have been proposed and empirically evaluated. As this is time-consuming, costly, and reduces the possible design parameters, we employed multi-objective Bayesian optimization to optimize the design of visualizations in AVs. In particular, we evaluated multi-session aspects involving iterative optimization. We optimized the design for passenger trust and perceived safety while minimizing cognitive load.
